# نبض GitHub في آخر 30 يومًا — GitHub 30-day pulse

Generated by `scripts/github_pulse.py build` from `data/github_pulse_snapshot.json` and `data/github_pulse_review.json`; do not edit by hand.

النافذة / window: **2026-09-26 → 2026-10-26** (720 hourly GH Archive files, 2,991,245 repositories with activity, 2,451 candidates enriched). القائمة: **1000** مستودعًا نشطًا (دُفع إليها أو صدر لها إصدار داخل النافذة).

**القرار / decision:** `mine` 387 · `watch` 354 · `reference` 242 · `transferred` 17

**الترخيص / licence class:** `permissive` 626 · `unrecognised` 208 · `copyleft` 139 · `none` 27

Importance = distinct people active in the window + 25 × log10(1 + total stars). Creative domains get a minimum share. `transferred` = an idea now lives in a skill tool or appendix (see the ideas table); `mine` = relevant and permissive; `reference` = relevant but copyleft/unrecognised/no licence; `watch` = general software; `excluded` = unsafe, never mined.

Limits: GH Archive has captured far fewer events since 2026 than before, so 30-day counts are a sample, good for ranking, not absolute numbers; repositories deps.dev has not indexed show no total stars; the domain is a keyword rule (fixes live in the review file).
